Final settlements, payments and run reversals stay with `approver` (default, OQ-36). **[svc + access; AT-33]**
    *Amended 2026-10-18 (P20(a), (b); to build):* the run approver is the entity's matrix row for `payroll.run`; seed `hr_officer` in **every** entity (P14); "contracted payroll needs no Managing Partner approval": no seed row and no fallback names `managing_partner` for runs. Final settlements, payments and run reversals: their own matrix activities (seed `approver`, OQ-36). The `hr_officer` never self-approves (access R58: only the top authority).
51. **Salary banks and WPS (P15).** `PayrollSettings.salary_bank` is one of Riyad Bank, SNB, Al Rajhi Bank, Bank AlJazira, Alinma Bank; another bank is refused. Each bank has its own WPS format parameter (`wps.format.<bank>`, layout and encoding per bank **[CC-verify: per-bank format not in HRSD sources]**; Ministry fields verified, R31); `wps_format` must be that bank's; generation is refused while that parameter is missing (or unverified with `HR_REQUIRE_VERIFIED_PARAMETERS=True`). `remuneration` staff are never put in a WPS file (no labour regulation); they are paid by bank transfer recorded as `PayrollPayment`. **[svc; AT-34]**
    *Amended 2026-10-17 (P16, P18):* the WPS exclusion of `remuneration` staff is decided (not in the Saudi labour system). The five banks' file formats will be supplied by the project owner; until then the `wps.format.<bank>` parameters cannot be completed and generation is refused as above (OQ-10); nothing else in payroll waits for them.

### Labour Law / WPS check 2026-10-17 (`docs/compliance/hr-payroll-labour-law-2026-10-17.md`)
52. **Overtime statutory caps.** For `ksa_employee`: (a) Σ approved overtime hours in the employee's year (per hr `calendar_basis`) ≤ `overtime.max_hours_year` = 720; an input that would exceed it is refused unless `worker_consent_ref` is stored (Regs Art. 22; downloaded 2026-10-17); (b) in the exceptional cases of Art. 106, hours per `overtime_date` ≤ 10 a day and ≤ 60 in the week, refused above (Labour Law Art. 106; downloaded 2026-10-17). Inputs need `overtime_date` for (b). **[svc; AT-35]**
53. **Mudad deadlines and alerts.** For each approved `in_country` run of an entity with WPS, if no `MudadSubmission` is recorded, alerts (en/ar) go to the payroll officer and hr_officer on day 7 of the following month (default, before Mudad's own day-10 e-mail), on day 10 and on day 15; Mudad refers to inspection on day 20 (WPS-G). A justification recorded in `MudadSubmission.justifications` is due within 10 days of Mudad's notice; the worker's response is due within 3 days (WPS-G; downloaded 2026-10-17). **[job + svc; AT-39]**
54. **Fines fund (if ever supported, OQ-8).** Disciplinary fines would credit a workers' fines-fund liability, never income, kept in a fines register and used only for the benefit of the establishment's workers (Labour Law Art. 73; Regs Art. 70; downloaded 2026-10-17). Not built: any fine input is refused today. **[svc; AT-37]**
    *Amended 2026-10-17:* WPS covers all private-sector workers, Saudi and expatriate; only "expatriate workers outside the Kingdom" and workers on probation are excluded from the compliance rate (WPS-G; downloaded 2026-10-17). Whether `remuneration` persons are workers at all (Labour Law Art. 2, 7, 90) is **[Needs expert sign-off: legal]** (OQ-38; hr OQ-38); if they are, SAR pay through accredited banks (Art. 90), WPS, EOSB and leave apply and P8/P9 must be revisited by the Managing Partner. The exclusion above stays as built default until then.
    *Amended 2026-10-17 (P16):* decided: remuneration staff work outside Saudi Arabia and are not workers under the Saudi Labour Law; the WPS exclusion is final (consistent with WPS-G excluding "expatriate workers outside the Kingdom"). OQ-38 resolved.

### GOSI check 2026-10-17 (`docs/compliance/gosi-check-2026-10-17.md`)
55. **GOSI age limits (report item R21a).** For Saudi `ksa_employee` (and Saudi `remuneration` once OQ-39 allows), using hr `date_of_birth` and `gosi_first_coverage_date`:
    - (a) no annuities lines if the age on `gosi_first_coverage_date` ≥ `gosi.legal_age` for the cohort ("must be below the legal age of entitlement on the date he becomes subject", SIL Art. 14(2); `new_m273` 65, Art. 16(1));
    - (b) no SANED lines if the age on `gosi_first_coverage_date` ≥ `gosi.saned_max_entry_age` = 64 (SIL Art. 43);
    - (c) SANED stops from the month the legal pension age is reached (contributions "suspended", Art. 43); the exact first month without SANED is `unverified` **[CC-verify: SIL Regulations not supplied]**;
    - (d) annuities continue for a contributor working past the legal age (Art. 17(4));
    - (e) `legacy_m33`: SANED per UIL Art. 3 (under 59 at entry into force; ends at 60) and the legal age per Clause Fifth or the old law: rows `unverified` outside the Clause Fifth group **[CC-verify: SIL M/33 (as amended) not supplied]**.
    Missing `gosi_first_coverage_date` for a Saudi: run warning (en/ar) and calculation refused for that employee, as for a missing cohort (3.4). **[svc + parameter; AT-44]**
    *Amended 2026-10-17 (second GOSI check, SIL M/33 booklet 2025; UIL booklet 2025):*
    - (a) the entry-age test applies to `new_m273` only. For `legacy_m33` (uncompensated earlier periods by definition) annuities apply at any age: a returning worker who was not a pensioner "is subject whatever his age" (SIL M/33 Art. 4(2)(b), p.5; F7, GOSI guide, secondary). A pensioner returning to work: OQ-41 **[Needs expert sign-off: legal]**.
    - (e) SANED part: tag removed; `legacy_m33` SANED is mandatory if the worker was "under 59" when the law first applied to him, suspended at 60 while still employed, and covers anyone mandatorily in annuities (UIL Art. 3(1)–(3), booklet p.3); verifiable. Legal age for other `legacy_m33` contributors: 60 with ≥ 120 months; before 60 with ≥ 300 months once no longer covered (SIL M/33 Art. 38(1)(a), (b), p.21); verifiable. The Clause Fifth group keeps its schedule.
    **[svc + parameter; AT-44, AT-48]**
56. **GOSI gating summary.** For each payslip, the calculation is refused (en/ar, naming the employee code and reason) when: (a) a Saudi `ksa_employee` has no `cohort` or no `gosi_first_coverage_date` (3.4, R55); (b) `nationality_class=gcc` (R21(h)); (c) a `remuneration` run contains a Saudi national (R41, OQ-39). Other employees of the same run are calculated; the run cannot be submitted while any payslip is refused. **[svc; AT-43, AT-45, AT-46]**
    *Amended 2026-10-17 (P19(b)):* (c) now reads: a Saudi `remuneration` person without `gosi_employment_relationship` (hr R69); added (d): a Saudi `local_entity_employee` of a GCC-state entity until the GCC extension parameters are verified (R41).
    *Amended 2026-10-19 (compliance C-A2):* "the run cannot be submitted while any payslip is refused" is relaxed: the payroll officer may submit with `exclude_refused`; refused payslips post nothing and take no input or installment, the run carries an en/ar warning, and their employees are paid by an off-cycle run once fixed (§10.5).

### Second GOSI check 2026-10-17, legacy cohort (`docs/compliance/gosi-check-legacy-2026-10-17.md`)
57. **GOSI wage-change alert.** When an `EmploymentContract` version of a `ksa_employee` is approved and it changes any `counts_for_gosi` component (amount added, changed or removed) against the version before it, the service sends an en/ar alert to the entity's payroll officers: "update the GOSI registered wage for {employee_code} from {effective_date}". The GOSI wage "must be amended in light of any change during the insurance year" (GOSI Form 7 item 3, GOSI guide, secondary); registering an incorrect wage is fined per contributor (violations fines rules Aug 2024, item 3). If `effective_date` is more than 3 months before the approval date, the alert also warns (en/ar) that a GOSI correction can go back at most 3 months before the request (F7). The ERP does not change the GOSI record; payroll still computes on the contract wage. Both cohorts. **[svc (hr contract approval hook) + alert; AT-49]**
58. **No catch-up employee deduction.** For `legacy_m33`: if the employer did not deduct the employee's GOSI share when paying the wage, "he may not deduct it afterwards in any form" (SIL M/33 Art. 19(4), booklet p.12). So an employee GOSI share that belongs to a month whose wage was already paid (e.g. a cohort, rate or wage correction found later) is never deducted in a later run or in the final settlement: the run books it as employer cost (Dr 5110, with the employer share, Cr 2340) and shows an en/ar warning naming Art. 19(4). A retro **wage** difference paid in the current run (R14) is paid now, so the employee share on that retro amount is still deducted in the current run. `new_m273`: the new law Art. 9(1) has no such sentence; the same rule applies as default **[Needs expert sign-off: legal]** (OQ-43). **[svc; AT-50]**
    *Amended 2026-10-17 (P19(j); resolves OQ-43; to build):* for `new_m273` a missed employee share **may be deducted later** with notification to the employee: the next regular run adds a `gosi_catch_up` deduction (within the R18 caps; a remainder moves to the following run) and writes a `GosiCatchUpNotice` (en/ar text on the payslip and an in-app notice to the employee when self-service exists; until then the payroll officer records how the employee was notified); a catch-up still open when employment ends goes to the final settlement, with the same notice. `legacy_m33` keeps the rule above (never deducted later, SIL M/33 Art. 19(4)). **[svc; AT-56]**
59. **Maternity benefit vs wage.** GOSI pays a maternity benefit "on behalf of the employer" to Saudi and resident contributors in the OH branch, under the old or the new law, for births from July 2025: 100% of the average contributory wage of the 12 months before birth, for 3 months (+1 for a sick or disabled child), with ≥ 12 months of contributions in the last 36; it "may not be combined with the wage" (GOSI maternity benefit guide 2025, p.3, GOSI guide, secondary). Whether the employer still owes the difference between the GOSI benefit and the full wage of hr 3.7 `MATERNITY`, and whether contributions continue, is **[Needs expert sign-off: legal]** (OQ-44). **Default until decided:** for a `ksa_employee` with an approved `MATERNITY` leave whose birth (`event_date`) is on or after 2025-07-01 and which overlaps the period, the run shows an en/ar warning naming the guide and **holds that employee's payslip**: no earnings, deduction or GOSI lines for her in the run, status `held` on the payslip, nothing posted for her. Other employees are calculated and the run may be submitted and approved. Held pay is settled by an off-cycle run (R6) once OQ-44 is decided; until then an off-cycle run including her is refused (en/ar). GOSI eligibility (12 of 36 months) is decided by GOSI, not computed by the ERP. **[svc; AT-51]**
    *Withdrawn 2026-10-17 — see Decision P19(a) (replaced by R61).* The "payslip held, no lines" default and the off-cycle refusal are removed when R61 is built.
60. **Wage increase after 50, `legacy_m33`.** When an approved contract version gives a `legacy_m33` contributor aged 50 or over (on the version's `effective_date`, from hr `date_of_birth`) a `counts_for_gosi` increase, the run shows an en/ar warning: contributions on the increase are payable "only within the ratio set by the Regulations" (SIL M/33 Art. 19(2), booklet p.12). Parameter `gosi.legacy_increase_after_50_ratio` is empty and `unverified` **[CC-verify: SIL Regulations not supplied]**; until it is set, payroll computes contributions on the full contract wage and the warning says so. **[svc + parameter; AT-52]**

### Project owner / CFO decision pack 2026-10-17 (P19; all to build)
61. **Maternity: GOSI compensation and top-up (P19(a); replaces R59).** For a `ksa_employee` on approved `MATERNITY` leave whose birth is on or after 2025-07-01, payroll reads the GOSI case recorded by HR (hr 3.7 note) and, for the days of the period inside the leave:
    - (a) **`gosi_confirmed`** (GOSI confirmed the compensation, its period and monthly amount entered from GOSI with `gosi_reference`): the contract earnings are **suspended only for the days covered by the confirmed compensation**; a `maternity_top_up` earning = max(0, full pay for those days − the GOSI amount for the same days), where full pay = the actual wage (components with `counts_for_eosb`, hr 3.3) / 30 per day (Art. 151 "full pay"). Days of the leave not covered by GOSI are paid in full as usual.
    - (b) **`pending_gosi`** (eligibility not yet confirmed): she is **not classified unpaid** (no unpaid-leave deduction, no service suspension, no leave-balance change); the salary for those days is neither paid nor deducted in the run but carried as `pending_gosi` on the payslip (amount shown, nothing posted for it); an urgent en/ar alert goes to the entity's hr_officers and the legal escalation contact (hr OQ-44); a temporary support payment may be added only as a `maternity_support` input with `legal_clearance_ref` approved by the approver (A). When the case is confirmed or refused, an off-cycle run (R6) settles the carried days under (a) or (c).
    - (c) **`gosi_refused`** (GOSI refused, e.g. fewer than 12 months of contributions): no GOSI compensation exists, so the employer's full pay for the 12 weeks (Art. 151) is paid for those days, once the hr_officer records the GOSI refusal reference (default, OQ-46).
    - (d) **Never full salary together with confirmed GOSI compensation** for the same days unless the payslip has a `legal_clearance_ref` (written legal clearance) approved by the approver; otherwise the calculation refuses it (en/ar).
    - (e) **All other legally due payments continue**: other earnings, approved inputs, EOSB accrual, loan or bai' ajel instalments within R18, leave accrual (hr R30).
    - (f) **GOSI contributions** continue on her registered contributory wage during the leave (assumption by the legal adviser, not confirmed from an official source, OQ-44(b)); an employee share that the period's net pay cannot cover is deducted later per R58 (`new_m273`) or borne by the employer (`legacy_m33`, Art. 19(4)).
    The run is submitted and approved as usual; her payslip is never `held`. **[svc + hr; AT-53, AT-54]**
    *Amended 2026-10-19 (compliance C-B10):* (f) is sourced: contributions continue during "the leaves provided for in the employment rules or regulations to which the contributor is subject" (GOSI Regs Art. 20(3)(b)); the "assumption ... not confirmed" wording is withdrawn. An employee share the net pay cannot cover is booked to 2340 in full that month (§10.5, C-B7).
    *Confirmed 2026-10-18 (P20(d), (e)):* (c) is decided: when GOSI **rejects** the maternity claim, the company pays **full salary** for the leave days (once the hr_officer records the refusal reference; days carried as `pending_gosi` are settled by off-cycle run at full pay); resolves OQ-46. (f) is decided: GOSI contributions **continue** during the GOSI maternity benefit on her registered contributory wage (no longer an assumption; the Regulations reference is still to be read by the compliance-checker, OQ-44(b) note). No code change beyond R61 as written. **[svc; AT-62, AT-63]**
62. **GOSI wage of Saudi remuneration staff (P19(b)).** For a `subordinate` Saudi `remuneration` person, the GOSI wage = the month's `REMUNERATION` amount converted to SAR at the run's rate on `entry_date` (R42), within the floor (SAR 1,500.00) and the cap (SAR 45,000.00) of R20 (default; whether the Regulations define the base differently: **[CC-verify: SIL Regulations not supplied]**, OQ-45). Employer annuities post Dr 5110 / Cr 2340 in SAR; the employee share is a SAR deduction converted into the payslip currency at the same rate. **[svc; AT-55]**
63. **Employee cost items for recharges (P19(c)).** Costs paid outside payroll that belong to one employee (medical insurance, iqama renewal, work permit and visa fees, other government fees and benefits) are recorded as `EmployeeCostItem`s per employee and month, each referencing its source document (vendor bill line or journal entry of the same entity), entered by the payroll officer and approved by the approver (A, not the creator). They post nothing (the cost is already booked by the source document) and feed only the secondment cost report (R35). An item's month is the source document's accounting month (default, billing OQ-37). **[svc; AT-59]**

## 5. Accounting

Codes from `ledger/coa_template.py`. Existing: 1100 Cash and bank, 2300 Accrued expenses, 2500 End-of-service provision, 5100 Salaries and benefits, 5110 GOSI, 5700 Bank charges. **Proposed, CFO to approve (not added to the template until approved; P6, OQ-12).** Codes chosen to avoid every code in `ledger/coa_template.py` and those already proposed in invoicing.md §5 (1330, 2220, 2310 deferred income, 2320 security deposits, 4120 to 4310, 5910, 5920) and ledger-fx.md (3400, 4700, 4710, 5750, 5760, 5770; 1101 example sub-account):

| Code | name_en | name_ar [AR-review] | Type | Flags | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Existing proposed 2220 (WHT payable, invoicing.md §5) is used for remuneration WHT (E8); FX differences use ledger-fx proposed 4700 / 5750. All stay **proposed, CFO to approve** (OQ-12).

Registered types (`PayrollConfig.ready()`): `PayrollRun` (post `payroll.approve_run`, reverse `payroll.reverse_run`), `PayrollPayment` (`payroll.approve_payment`), `EmployeeLoan` (`payroll.approve_loan`), `FinalSettlement` (`payroll.approve_final_settlement`); *added 2026-10-17:* `DeferredSaleContract` (`payroll.approve_deferred_sale`), `DeferredProfitRecognition` (`payroll.approve_profit_recognition`); `PayrollRun` posting permission held by `hr_officer` (R50), `EmployeeLoan` by `managing_partner` (R46); each with `build_lines`, `creator_field="created_by"`, and `pending_documents` for submitted runs. Figures below use the **unverified** seed GOSI rates ~~[CC-verify: GOSI source blocked]~~ (*amended 2026-10-17:* E1's S1 is `legacy_m33`; its 9% + 9% annuities and 0.75% + 0.75% SANED stay unverified **[CC-verify: SIL M/33 (as amended) not supplied]**, **[CC-verify: UIL Art. 7 says 1%+1%; amendment not supplied]**; OH 2% and the cap are verifiable, SIL Art. 8(2), 29(1); a `new_m273` Saudi in March 2027 pays annuities 20% (10% + 10%), see AT-42) and the EOSB table verified 2026-10-17 (Labour Law Art. 84, 85); GOSI amounts change when those parameters are verified. *Amended 2026-10-17 (second GOSI check):* E1's S1 figures (`legacy_m33`, GOSI wage 12,500.00: employee 1,218.75, employer 1,468.75) are now backed by sources: annuities 9% + 9% and OH 2% (SIL M/33 Art. 18, GOSI booklet 2025 p.11), cap (Art. 19(1)(b)), SANED 0.75% + 0.75% (UIL Art. 7 as amended, booklet 2025 p.4; SANED guide 2025, secondary); HOUSING counts per GOSI Form 7 (secondary). The rows still need a human verifier (hr R7). R58 adds Dr 5110 / Cr 2340 for a missed employee share that may not be deducted; R59 held payslips add nothing to E1.

**E1. Monthly run, ALM, March 2027, dated 2027-03-31.** Saudi employee S1 (*amended 2026-10-17:* cohort `legacy_m33`, seed rates unverified): BASIC 10,000.00, HOUSING 2,500.00, TRANSPORT 1,000.00 (GOSI wage 12,500.00; employee 9% + 0.75% = 1,218.75; employer 9% + 0.75% + 2% = 1,468.75). Non-Saudi N1: BASIC 6,000.00, HOUSING 1,500.00, TRANSPORT 500.00 (GOSI wage 7,500.00; employer 2% = 150.00); loan installment 500.00. EOSB accrual: S1 562.50, N1 333.33.

| Account | Dr | Cr |
|---|---|---|
| 5100 Salaries and benefits | 21,500.00 | |
| 5110 GOSI (employer) | 1,618.75 | |
| 5120 EOSB expense (proposed) | 895.83 | |
| 2340 GOSI payable (proposed) | | 2,837.50 |
| 1230 Employee loans and advances (proposed) | | 500.00 |
| 2330 Salaries payable (proposed) | | 19,781.25 |
| 2500 End-of-service provision | | 895.83 |
| **Total** | **24,014.58** | **24,014.58** |

A negative EOSB accrual swaps 5120 and 2500. Retro, overtime and one-off earnings add to the 5100 (or component account) line; unpaid-leave and sick reductions lower it.

**E2. GOSI payment** (2,837.50): Dr 2340 2,837.50 / Cr 1100 2,837.50.

**E3. Net salary payment** (19,781.25): Dr 2330 19,781.25 / Cr 1100 (settings bank account) 19,781.25. Bank charges, if any, are a separate bank document (Dr 5700).

**E4. Loan disbursement** (3,000.00, interest-free): Dr 1230 3,000.00 / Cr 1100 3,000.00. Repayment through E1 (Cr 1230). No interest or fee line ever exists.

**E5. Final settlement, N1 resigns after 4 years** (wage basis 8,000.00; full award 16,000.00 = provision balance; resignation fraction 1/3 → EOSB 5,333.33 (Labour Law Art. 85; downloaded 2026-10-17; N1 is on a fixed-term contract, hr R16); 10 days leave 2,666.67; loan balance 1,000.00):

| Account | Dr | Cr |
|---|---|---|
| 2500 End-of-service provision (utilise 5,333.33, release 10,666.67) | 16,000.00 | |
| 5100 Salaries and benefits (leave encashment) | 2,666.67 | |
| 5120 EOSB expense (release of excess provision) | | 10,666.67 |
| 1230 Employee loans and advances | | 1,000.00 |
| 2330 Salaries payable | | 7,000.00 |
| **Total** | **18,666.67** | **18,666.67** |

Payment as E3 (kind `final_settlement`). If the award exceeds the provision, the shortfall is Dr 5120.

**E6. Run reversal** (R13): `reverse_entry` mirror of E1, dated in an open period, before any payment.

**E7. Secondment recharge**: none in payroll. Billing invoices the receiving entity (billing B3: Dr 1210 counterparty / Cr 4210 / Cr 2200 in the seconding entity).

**No entry** for: inputs, calculation, submission, payslips, WPS files, Mudad records, GOSI reconciliation, draft loans or settlements, leave accrual (until hr OQ-6).

## 6. Integrations

| System | Direction | What flows | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hr` (internal) | In | Contracts, components, leave, sick tiers, EOSB calculation, terminations, statutory parameters | hr.md |
| `ledger` (internal) | Out | `create_entry(post=True, grant, document)` for 4 registered types; `reverse_entry`; `pending_documents` | access R34 to R36 |
| `billing` (internal) | Out (report) | Secondment cost report per assignment (no PD beyond `employee_code`) | R35; OQ-15 |
| GOSI ~~**[CC-verify: GOSI source blocked]**~~ (*amended 2026-10-17:* checked against SIL M/273 and UIL M/18) | none / manual | Contribution report per month; GOSI invoice amount entered for reconciliation (and the late fine, E14); employee registration on the GOSI portal. *Amended 2026-10-17:* registration and data accuracy are fined per contributor, "not exceeding fifty thousand riyals", doubled on repeat; registering "a person not employed thereby" is also fined (SIL Art. 59(1), (2); legacy UIL Art. 25: ≤ SAR 10,000 per subscriber); this bears on Saudi remuneration staff (OQ-39, OQ-40). Registration and payment deadlines: `gosi.registration_days`, `gosi.payment_due_day` **[CC-verify: SIL Regulations not supplied]**. *Amended 2026-10-17 (second GOSI check):* `legacy_m33` fines: "not more than 10,000 riyals, doubled on repeat, multiplied by the number of contributors"; registering a person "who does not work for him": that fine or up to twice the contributions for the registered period, whichever is higher; no fine after 5 years (SIL M/33 Art. 62(1), (2), (7), booklet p.31; UIL Art. 25(1)); `gosi.violation_fine_max` gets a `legacy_m33` row. Per-contributor schedule of the violations fines rules (Aug 2024, Arabic, governs over the 2023 English v02) seeded as reference data `gosi.violation_schedule`, `unverified` (which law it is issued under is unclear): no real working relationship (contributions + SAR 300 to 20,000); leaving reason not matching the real reason (SAR 500 to 6,000; hr R65); incorrect wage (SAR 500 to 12,000; R57); failure to register a real worker (SAR 10,000 first time, 20,000 on repeat, per contributor); false data leading to benefits (30% / 60% / 90% of the amount paid, capped 50k / 100k / 200k). Registration deadline now `gosi.registration_deadline` (R10); payment day 15 (R22). Items 1 and 4 also bear on Saudi remuneration staff (OQ-40) | OQ-9 |
| Mudad / WPS | Out (file to bank) / In (bank-processed file) / Out (manual upload to Mudad) | *Amended 2026-10-17 (WPS-G, WPS-P; downloaded 2026-10-17):* ERP salary file (Ministry header + content fields, R31) to the bank; bank-processed file back; that file uploaded manually in the Mudad compliance system (only route); reference, status and justifications entered manually (R32, R53). No API documented **[CC-verify: no Mudad API in HRSD sources]** | OQ-10 **B** for file generation (per-bank layout) |
| Banks | Out (file via Mudad or bank portal) / In (manual) | Salary file; payment confirmation recorded as `PayrollPayment` | Bank module later. *Amended 2026-10-17 (P15):* Riyad Bank, SNB, Al Rajhi Bank, Bank AlJazira, Alinma Bank, one format each **[CC-verify: per-bank format not in HRSD sources]** (R51); remuneration paid by (foreign-currency) transfer, outside WPS |
| ZATCA (WHT) | Out (data) | Remuneration WHT in the monthly WHT return data via invoicing (R43) | OQ-29, OQ-30. *Added 2026-10-17* |
| `invoicing` (internal) | In / Out | Bai' ajel asset purchase as a vendor bill (1250); VAT/e-invoice for the sale to the employee if required (OQ-35); WHT categories and return data (R43) | *Added 2026-10-17*. *Amended 2026-10-17 (P19(k)):* the sale invoice at 15% is required (invoicing R112) |
| Qiwa | none | — (contract documentation is HR's, hr R61; Regs Art. 18) | hr OQ-21 |
| ZATCA | none | Payroll is not a VAT supply; secondment invoices go through billing/invoicing | — (*amended 2026-10-17:* except WHT data and possibly the bai' ajel sale, rows below) |
| Legacy system (Falcon) or another source system | In (one-off) | Opening EOSB provision per employee (`opening` rows tied to 2500), open loans, YTD | migration-agent; OQ-26. *Amended 2026-10-17 (P19(o)):* Almiras's source is Falcon |

## 7. Permissions

Roles `payroll_officer` and `hr_officer` are **proposed** (hr OQ-2). No permission is **Ag** (R38).
*Amended 2026-10-17 (P14, P12, P11):* both roles are **decided**, granted by `managing_partner` (hr R57, access A21). Changed holders: `payroll.approve_run` → `hr_officer` (R50); `payroll.approve_loan` → `managing_partner` (R46). New: `payroll.approve_overtime` (A): `managing_partner` or proposed `department_head` per hr R55 (R45); `payroll.create_deferred_sale` / `payroll.submit_deferred_sale`: payroll_officer; `payroll.approve_deferred_sale_terms` (A) and `payroll.approve_deferred_sale` (A, P): `managing_partner`; `payroll.prepare_profit_recognition` / `payroll.approve_profit_recognition` (A, P): payroll_officer or accountant / approver (default, OQ-36). The tables below are amended accordingly.

| Codename | Flags | Holders (default) |
|---|---|---|
| `payroll.view` (aggregates, masked) | | payroll_officer, approver, accountant, viewer |
| `payroll.view_payslip` (individual pay) | | payroll_officer, approver (logged) |
| `payroll.manage_inputs` / `payroll.approve_input` | — / A | payroll_officer, hr_officer / payroll_officer (not own entry, not own pay) |
| `payroll.prepare_run` / `payroll.submit_run` | | payroll_officer |
| `payroll.approve_run` | A, P | ~~approver~~ hr_officer (P14, R50) |
| `payroll.reverse_run` | P | approver (not the preparer) |
| `payroll.create_loan` / `payroll.approve_loan` | — / A, P | payroll_officer / ~~approver~~ managing_partner (P12, R46) |
| `payroll.approve_overtime` | A | managing_partner (route `managing_partner`) / department_head, proposed (route `department_head`) (R45) |
| `payroll.create_deferred_sale` / `payroll.approve_deferred_sale_terms` / `payroll.approve_deferred_sale` | — / A / A, P | payroll_officer / managing_partner / managing_partner (R47) |
| `payroll.prepare_profit_recognition` / `payroll.approve_profit_recognition` | — / A, P | payroll_officer, accountant / approver (default, OQ-36) |
| `payroll.prepare_final_settlement` / `payroll.approve_final_settlement` | — / A, P | payroll_officer / approver |
| `payroll.record_payment` / `payroll.approve_payment` | — / A, P | payroll_officer / approver |
| `payroll.generate_wps_file` / `payroll.record_mudad_submission` | S! (file holds PD) / — | payroll_officer |
| `payroll.manage_settings` | | approver |
| `payroll.view_cost_reports` (secondment cost, GOSI, EOSB reports without PD) | | accountant, approver, viewer |

| Action | payroll_officer | hr_officer | approver (CFO) | accountant | viewer | managing_partner | Agent / system |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Enter inputs | Y | Y | | | | | No |
| Prepare, calculate, submit run | Y | | | | | | No |
| Approve (= post) ~~run, loan,~~ settlement, payment (*amended 2026-10-17*: run → next row, loan → row after) | | | Y (not preparer/submitter/modifier; R39) | | | Fallback (access OQ-1) | No |
| *Amended 2026-10-17:* approve (= post) run (R50) | No | Y (not preparer; not own inputs) | No | | | Fallback | No |
| *Amended 2026-10-17:* approve loan, bai' ajel terms and sale (R46, R47) | No | | No | | | Y (not creator) | No |
| *Added 2026-10-17:* approve overtime (R45) | No | | | | | Y where route = `managing_partner`; elsewhere the department head (proposed role `department_head`) | No |
| Reverse run | | | Y | | | | No |
| Generate WPS file | Y | | | | | | No |
| See payslips | Y | | Y | | | | No |
| See cost reports | | | Y | Y | Y | | No |

*Added 2026-10-17 (P19), to build:* record the GOSI maternity case: hr_officer (hr 3.7 note); approve a `maternity_support` input or a `legal_clearance_ref` (R61): approver (A, not the creator); enter / approve `EmployeeCostItem`s and the fully loaded secondment cost report (R35, R63): payroll_officer / approver (A); record a `GosiCatchUpNotice` (R58): payroll_officer. Agents and system users: none.
*Amended 2026-10-18 (P20), to build:* every approver named in this section reads "eligible under the entity's authority matrix, through the approval engine" (access R57, R61); the seed rows are the holders above (Almiras: MP for overtime, loans, bai' ajel; every entity: `hr_officer` for runs, never the MP). Self-approval only by the entity's top authority (access R58). The MP acts outside Almiras only with a membership there (no cascade, access R6).

## 8. Acceptance tests

Fixtures: ALM (SA, SAR, Sharia enforced), SUB (SA, SAR), EGY (EG, EGP); template chart plus proposed accounts 1230, 2330, 2340, 5120 (test fixture only); 2027 periods open unless stated; hr employees S1 (Saudi, as E1) and N1 (non-Saudi, as E1) in ALM, S2 in SUB; unverified seed parameters with `HR_REQUIRE_VERIFIED_PARAMETERS=False` unless stated. Users: P (payroll_officer ALM), P2 (payroll_officer ALM), C (approver ALM), C2 (approver SUB), A (accountant ALM), V (viewer), MP, agent. Anonymised data only.
*Amended 2026-10-17 (P12, P14):* fixtures add H and H2 (hr_officer ALM), DH (department head of SUB's OPS department, proposed `department_head` role in SUB), remuneration employee R1 in ALM (USD, residence EG), proposed accounts 1240, 1241, 1250, 4610 and 2220. Where AT-5, AT-7, AT-13 and AT-24 say "C approves" a **run**, read "H approves" (C is refused, AT-33); in AT-24 the employee-approver is H instead of C. In AT-17, "C approves" the loan reads "MP approves". Payments, final settlements and reversals stay with C.

| # | Given | When | Then |
|---|---|---|---|
| AT-1 | Runs in ALM and SUB | P lists runs; opens SUB run; a build_lines variant targets SUB's 2330; ALM run tries to include S2 | ALM only; SUB → 404; ledger refuses other-entity account; S2 refused (R1, R2, R30) |
| AT-2 | NEW entity set up as data with payroll enabled; EGY enabled | Run March for NEW; for EGY | NEW runs with no code change; EGY refused "jurisdiction not configured" (en/ar) (R3, R4) |
| AT-3 | Input amount as float; S1 contract in USD (fixture forced) | Save input; calculate | Float refused; calculation refused naming the currency (R5) |
| AT-4 | March regular run approved | Create a second regular March run; off-cycle without `reason_ar`; set `status="approved"` directly on a draft | Refused; refused; refused (model, and DB trigger on PostgreSQL) (R6, R7) |
| AT-5 | Run `submitted` | Approve after an hr leave approval changed S1's unpaid days | Refused "recalculate" (en/ar), grant unused; after recalculation and resubmission C approves (R8) |

Questions for legal: (a) does Art. 88 cover a bai' ajel receivable and an employer loan; (b) do the 10% and 50% caps apply at final settlement; (c) are bai' ajel installments Art. 92(4) or do they need written consent; (d) is a signed undertaking enforceable; (e) cap bases: actual wage vs due wage (R18). Bai' ajel: same treatment only with the signed undertaking in the contract (`final_dues_undertaking`, R49), answering (d) as a condition. | OQ-44 | *Added 2026-10-17 (second GOSI check).* **[Needs expert sign-off: legal]**, then **Managing Partner decision**: GOSI maternity benefit vs the Labour Law's 12 weeks of full pay (hr 3.7, Art. 151). GOSI pays 100% of the 12-month average contributory wage for 3 months (+1) "on behalf of the employer", for births from July 2025, and it "may not be combined with the wage" (GOSI maternity guide 2025, secondary). (a) Does the employer owe the difference between the GOSI benefit and the full wage (contributory wage excludes transport and other allowances)? (b) Do contributions continue during the benefit period? (c) What does payroll pay while GOSI eligibility (≥ 12 of the last 36 months) is pending or refused? (d) How is held pay released? | Legal + Managing Partner | **GL** for paying employees on maternity leave with births from 2025-07 (their payslips are held; nothing else blocked) | Run warning and payslip held for that employee (R59); rest of the run proceeds. **Resolved by P19(a) (2026-10-17):** (a) yes, a top-up to full pay; (c) pending cases are not unpaid, carried and escalated, support only with legal clearance; (d) settled by off-cycle when confirmed or refused (R61). No longer GL once R61 is built. Still open: (b) contributions during the benefit period: **assumed** to continue on the contributory wage, not confirmed from an official source (to verify in the Regulations); the refused-case treatment: OQ-46. **(b) resolved by P20(e) (2026-10-18):** contributions continue (decision); the compliance-checker still records the Regulations reference when uqn.gov.sa is readable |
| OQ-45 | *Added 2026-10-17 (P19(b), R62).* GOSI wage of a subordinate Saudi remuneration person paid in a foreign currency: is the whole remuneration the "basic wage" of SIL Art. 8, and which SAR rate and date apply? **[CC-verify: SIL Regulations not supplied]** | compliance-checker + tax adviser | D | Whole `REMUNERATION` amount at the run's rate on `entry_date`, within floor and cap (R62) |
| OQ-46 | *Added 2026-10-17 (P19(a), R61).* GOSI refuses maternity compensation (e.g. under 12 months of contributions in the last 36): does the employer then pay the full 12 weeks itself (Art. 151), and from when? And what is the legal escalation contact (hr OQ-44)? | Legal + Managing Partner | D | Employer pays full pay for the leave days once the refusal reference is recorded (R61(c)). **Resolved by P20(d) (2026-10-18):** yes, full salary. Still open: legal escalation contact (hr OQ-44) |
| OQ-47 | *Added 2026-10-18 (P20(a), (c)).* Payroll approvers outside Almiras: loans, bai' ajel, overtime, final settlements, payments (each entity's matrix; access OQ-30, OQ-39). | Each CEO + project owner | D | Seed: loans and bai' ajel → `ceo`; overtime → `department_head` route; runs → `hr_officer`; settlements, payments, reversals → `approver` |

Does it hold for `legacy_m33`? | Legal | D | Home entity keeps paying; en/ar warning on the run and the secondment cost report |

## 10. As built: slice 1, KSA payroll end to end (2026-10-19)

Built on branch `claude/funny-davinci-7yd88s` by the module-builder on the owner's instruction of 2026-10-19 (owner answers I1: "start the payroll build now ... proceed on recommendations"), with the coordinator's instruction to apply the differences of the GOSI Implementing Regulations check (`docs/compliance/gosi-regulations-2026-10-19.md`, commit b851446). App `payroll`; logic in `payroll/services.py`; approval seam in `payroll/approvals.py`; parameters in `payroll/statutory_seed.py` (migration `payroll.0002`); PostgreSQL triggers in `payroll.0003`; permissions in access seed **V13** (migration `core.0022`).

### 10.1 Built
- **Settings and calendar (3.1, R3, R10, R44, P10):** `PayrollSettings` per entity (disabled until configured; `payroll_setup_entity`); cut-off 27th in-country, 6th of the following month out-of-country; an input's run is fixed at approval (`payroll_period`). Day basis `thirty` (day 31 carries no pay, the last day of February stands for the rest of a 30-day month, so a month is always 30) or `calendar` with `day_basis_ref`. OH override 2.00 < x ≤ 4.00 with GOSI reference and **from/to dates** (Regs Art. 22(3)), logged; in-kind housing exemption reference (Regs Art. 17(c)).
- **Runs (R6 to R13):** `draft → calculated → submitted (for review) → approved (= posted, P14) → paid`, `submitted → returned → calculated`, `approved → reversed`. The task's "reviewed" step is `submitted`; approval and posting are one step (P14). One live regular run per (entity, period, group); off-cycle needs `reason_ar` and named employees (model and validation only, see 10.4). Number `{entity}-PAY-{year}-{000001}` at approval. Approval recomputes the run in memory and compares a **fingerprint of the full result** (every line, refusal, rate, parameter id) with the one stored at calculation (R8: same effect as a hash of the sources; a change that does not change any amount does not force a recalculation); a mismatch refuses ("recalculate", en/ar) with the grant unused. Posting through `create_entry(post=True, grant, document=run)` with the registered `build_lines`; `pending_documents` blocks `close_period` while a run is submitted; reversal only through `reverse_run` (`reverse_guard`), only before an approved payment, dated in an open period; installments back to planned and EOSB rows mirrored. PostgreSQL triggers lock approved/paid/reversed runs, their payslips and lines, approved payments, loans once submitted, the EOSB sub-ledger (append-only) and WPS files (never deleted).
- **Approvers (P14, P20, access A24 to A26):** the authority matrix is not built; `payroll/approvals.py` holds the seed rows and checks the grant's roles: runs `hr_officer` only (the MP holds every A permission as fallback, OQ-1, but is refused for runs: "contracted payroll needs no MP approval"); overtime per `HrSettings.overtime_approval_route` (data): MP, or the user linked to the head of the employee's department holding the new role `department_head`; a head's own overtime or a department without an active head goes to the MP (hr OQ-30 default); loans and bai' ajel `managing_partner`; payments, reversals and maternity clearances `approver` (MP fallback). R39/R50: an approver whose own payslip has inputs (or retro), or who entered or approved an input of the run, is refused. Top-authority self-approval (access R58) waits for the matrix: nobody self-approves.
- **Earnings (R10, R14 to R17, R41):** prorated contract components per version in force each day; unpaid days from hr and `unpaid_days` inputs; sick tiers (official holidays paid in full); overtime `hours × (actual wage / days / daily_hours + 50% × basic / days / daily_hours)` with the 720-hour year cap (consent above it) and, for inputs flagged `art106_exceptional`, 10 a day / 60 a week (week Sunday to Saturday, default); one-off earnings, bonus. Leave encashment and disciplinary fines are input kinds that are always refused (Art. 109(2), R54). Leave days on full pay are paid as salary. **Retro (R14):** every earlier approved regular payslip of the group is re-examined against the versions approved now; positive differences per component are `retro` lines; a decrease is a warning only (recovery needs an approved deduction with written consent, Art. 92).
- **Maternity (R61):** `MaternityGosiCase` per approved MATERNITY leave (births from 2025-07-01), recorded by the hr_officer (`payroll.record_maternity_case`) instead of fields on the leave request (the hr leave request is service-guarded; the case is consumed only by payroll). Confirmed: contract pay suspended for the covered days, `maternity_top_up` = full pay − GOSI amount for those days; refused (with GOSI refusal reference): full pay; pending or not recorded: the days' pay is carried as `pending_gosi_amount` (not unpaid) with an urgent en/ar alert to hr_officers and the MP (legal escalation contact default, hr OQ-44); `maternity_support` inputs need `legal_clearance_ref` and the approver; full salary next to confirmed compensation only with an approved written clearance on the case, else the payslip is refused. GOSI continues on the registered wage (Regs Art. 20(3)(b)).
- **GOSI (R20 to R22, R41, R55 to R58, R60, R62 and the Regulations' differences, new rules R64 to R68):** rates per cohort from dated rows (new-law steps 9 / 9.5 / 10 / 10.5 / 11 each side from 2024-07-03, 2025-08-01, 2026-08-01, 2027-08-01, 2028-08-01: the August reading; the four July months read `gosi.annuities_step_start`, unverified); floor Saudi 1,500 / non-Saudi 400 before proration; cap 45,000 **across employers** (R68, Regs Art. 19: `Employee.gosi_other_employers_wage` from the GOSI record plus the same person's contracts in other group entities, pro rata); contributory wage = Regs Art. 17 closed list: components flagged `counts_for_gosi` (BASIC, cash HOUSING), COMMISSION and the new PROFIT_SHARE at **last year's monthly average** (`GosiVariableWageBase`, R65, Regs Art. 18(2); missing → payslip refused), in-kind housing at BASIC × 2 / 12 (`EmploymentContract.housing_in_kind`); wage of the version in force on the first employed day (R20(g) seed rule); **never reduced** by unpaid, sick or maternity days (R67, Regs Art. 20(1), (3)); **joining and leaving months on days of service** (R66, Regs Art. 20(2), `gosi.partial_month_divisor` = calendar, unverified); OH suspended for whole months between a personal exit and the return month (`GosiAbroadPeriod`, R64, Regs Art. 23); age tests on the GOSI-registered date of birth when entered (`Employee.gosi_registered_dob`, Regs Art. 9); Saudi remuneration staff by `Employee.gosi_employment_relationship` (subordinate: annuities only at the run's rate, R62; non-subordinate: none, note; missing: refused); GCC refused. **Catch-up (R58):** earlier months are recomputed on the wage now known; the share on a retro wage is deducted now; a missed `new_m273` share is a `gosi_catch_up` deduction within the caps with a `GosiCatchUpNotice` (en/ar on the payslip; the officer records how the employee was told); a missed `legacy_m33` share is employer cost (Art. 19(4)) with a warning. Warnings: unregistered (with the Regs Art. 6 and 16 texts), GOSI wage change ("update GOSI by the last day of the month, Regs Art. 6(2)(c); after that only at GOSI's discretion, Art. 21", replacing the F7 3-month text), legacy increase after 50, non-Saudi contract of 3 months or less, KSA-to-KSA secondment (Regs Art. 20(3)(a), **OQ-48**, home entity keeps paying).
- **Deductions (R18, R26, R46 to R49):** order: fixed one-off deductions (refuse the payslip if above 50% of the wage due), employee GOSI (reduced to the room left; the rest is caught up next month), loan installments ≤ 10% of the actual wage, bai' ajel installments (50% only, `deferred_sale.apply_loan_cap` False), catch-up shares; a reduced installment is re-planned as a carried installment after the last one; net < 0 refuses. Deduction bases: written consent (reference required), court order ≤ 1/4, damage ≤ 5 days' wage. Loans: interest-free in every entity (any interest/fee/profit/rate/penalty argument is refused with the Sharia message), schedule sums to the principal, advance = one installment, cap principal + outstanding ≤ 3 × BASIC at approval (stored), disbursement E4 posted at the MP's approval, rescheduling = new approved schedule version, skip refused, settled when fully deducted. Bai' ajel: request, MP terms approval, consent required, sale through `payroll.invoicing_port` (invoicing R112 not built: refused by default; the sale invoice's VAT 15% on cost + profit is charged to the employee, P20(h)), schedule of sale price + VAT, installments Cr 1240.
- **EOSB (R24, R25):** accrual = `hr.calculate_eosb` full award (employer termination) at the last employed day − sub-ledger balance; Dr 5120 / Cr 2500 (release reversed); tie-out warning when 2500 differs from the sub-ledger.
- **Remuneration (R41 to R43, R62):** `out_of_country` run in the contract currency at the ledger rate of the entry date; posted as matched pairs so SAR balances exactly (one earnings account per payslip); payment E8b: Dr 2330 settling the run's line at the carrying rate, Cr bank at the payment-date rate, Cr 2220 WHT in SAR, realised FX on 4700 / 5750; a WHT category is required on every remuneration payment (OQ-29 still open: no category is assumed); `wht_return_rows` is the seam for invoicing's WHT return (OQ-30, not wired).
- **Payments (R29):** net salaries per run and currency, GOSI per period; ≤ open amount; approver (A) posts; run `paid` when fully paid.
- **WPS / Mudad (R31, R32, R51, R53):** `generic_csv` (internal list) and `mudad_placeholder` (Ministry header and content fields of `wps.fields.mol`: MOL-BAS = BASIC, MOL-HALI = cash HOUSING, 0 for in-kind) are generated from approved runs; bank formats are refused until `wps.format.<bank>` exists (P18); KSA employees only; invalid IBAN → exception list; encrypted content, sha256, generation and downloads logged; bank-processed file required before a Mudad record; files kept indefinitely and nominated for a burn decision after 10 years by the `payroll_alerts` job (never deleted). Alerts: Mudad days 7 / 10 / 15, GOSI registration 25th and month end, GOSI payment on the 10th (due 15th, Regs Art. 24(1); 2% fine and Art. 25 waivers shown on the GOSI report).
- **Payslips and reports (R2, R33, R35, R40):** bilingual HTML payslip from stored lines (IBAN last 4 digits only, no ID number, "interest-free / قرض حسن" when a loan line exists, catch-up notices, WHT net-of-WHT for remuneration), each view logged; payroll register and bank transfer list (individual pay, logged), GOSI report and secondment cost report (codes only, payroll-only costs), GOSI reconciliation (R23, never posted).
- **HR additions (hr 3.2, 3.3, 3.4):** `gosi_employment_relationship` + `gosi_relationship_ref` (hr R69), `gosi_registered_dob`, `gosi_other_employers_wage`, `housing_in_kind` (refused with a cash HOUSING), seeded components COMMISSION and PROFIT_SHARE (counts for GOSI and EOSB). hr's `HR_PAYROLL_PORT` is now `payroll.hr_port.PayrollHrPort` (loan and bai' ajel balances on the settlement input).
- **Accounts:** 1230, 1240, 2330, 2340, 5120 added to the chart template and to existing charts (2350/5130 not added: leave accrual is not decided, hr OQ-6).
- **Egypt (R4, OQ-28):** `jurisdiction.active` rows for SA and EG and the Egypt research rows (labour, social insurance, salary tax, levies) are seeded `unverified`; an EG entity's run is refused "jurisdiction not configured" (local calculation not built); CN/AE are refused "jurisdiction not activated".

### 10.2 Defaults chosen in the build (team recommendations, owner I1)
- One-off earnings and deductions without a component book to 5100 (earnings: R19 default; deductions reduce salary cost); a deduction component's `expense_account` is its credit account. *To confirm with the accountant.*
- `hr_officer` holds `payroll.view_payslip` (it approves the run and must review individual pay); not in the §7 table.
- `payroll.generate_wps_file` is not flagged S!: with the run as document it would bar the preparer, the only seeded holder.
- AT-8 says 17/30 for 15 to 31 March; the build pays 16/30 so the two versions of March add up to 30/30 (AT-6's 20/30 for a joiner on the 11th fixes the day-31 rule). A version change in the middle of a month does not change that month's GOSI wage (R20(g) start-of-month seed rule); a change from the 1st of a past month carries retro GOSI shares.
- AT-37's 4,500.00 case: R18 reduces installments first; the payslip is refused only when the fixed one-off deductions alone exceed 50%.

### 10.3 Spec changes applied from the GOSI Regulations report (2026-10-19)
New rules (numbering of the report): **R64** OH abroad, **R65** commission and profit share at the previous year's average, **R66** joining and leaving months on days of service, **R67** leave and authorised absence do not reduce the GOSI wage, **R68** cap across employers. New parameters (all `unverified`): `gosi.partial_month_divisor`, `gosi.variable_wage_basis`, `gosi.oh_abroad_suspension`, `gosi.establishment_deadline_days`, `gosi.min_pension_months`, `gosi.early_pension_months`, the Clause Fifth table in `gosi.legal_age` (with the track rule "under 240 months and under 48y6m Gregorian on 2024-07-03"). New open question **OQ-48** *(legal)*: is a billing secondment between two KSA group entities an "إعارة" under Regs Art. 20(3)(a), moving GOSI registration and payment to the receiving entity? Default: home entity pays, warning on the run and the cost report. New acceptance tests AT-67 to AT-72 are tests in `payroll/test_gosi.py`.

### 10.4 Not in slice 1 (slice 2)
*Amended 2026-10-19 (review fixes, §10.5):* the final settlement and the off-cycle calculation are now built; the list below is what remains.
*Amended 2026-10-20 (slice 2, §10.8):* built now: the settlement correction (a later document, not an approval reversal) and the printable statement, the loan write-off, employee cost items and the approved fully loaded cost report, the bai' ajel sale invoice through invoicing and profit recognition on collection (E12, E13), remuneration retro and GOSI catch-up, the hourly GOSI gate, the GOSI fine helper, payment reversal, hr R68 and R69, the Egypt calculation. Still not built: off-cycle runs of the `out_of_country` group and of Egypt; per-bank WPS byte layouts (P18) and Mudad justifications; leave accrual (hr OQ-6 undecided: a liability report instead); the authority matrix, top-authority self-approval and the governance report (access build); the `ceo` role (access A28).
*Amended 2026-10-20 (§11):* slice 3 covers off-cycle runs of the `out_of_country` group and of Egypt, the leave liability and EOSB provision reports, Mudad justifications and payroll's side of the approval engine. Still outside: per-bank WPS byte layouts (P18), Egypt NOSI / ETA filings and payments, leave accrual postings (hr OQ-6), bai' ajel asset returns.
Final settlement: approval reversal (refused: corrections are a later document), the payslip-style print of a settlement, the write-off of an unrecovered loan (OQ-7: refused, warning) and the recognition of unearned bai' ajel profit on exit (E13, waits for E12); off-cycle runs of the `out_of_country` group (refused, en/ar); deferred profit recognition (E12, E13) and the bai' ajel sale invoice itself (waits for invoicing R112); employee cost items and the fully loaded, approved secondment cost report (R63, billing R71); remuneration retro and GOSI catch-up; per-bank WPS byte layouts (P18) and Mudad justifications; hourly-paid GOSI rules (Regs Art. 18(3)); GOSI late-fine entry helper (E14) and payment reversal; leave accrual (hr OQ-6); the authority matrix, top-authority self-approval and the governance report (access 3.8 to 3.10); hr R68 in hr itself (contract approval for non-active jurisdictions) and hr R69's refusal of a Saudi remuneration contract without the relationship; Egypt calculation (needs verified EG parameters, OQ-28).

### 10.5 Code-review and compliance fixes (2026-10-19, as built)
Built on the same branch from the code review and the Labour Law / GOSI / Mudad compliance review of slice 1, on the owner's instruction "proceed on recommendations, no questions". Every item has a regression test (`payroll/test_review_*.py`, `payroll/test_final_settlement.py`, `payroll/test_pg_concurrency.py`, `hr/test_iban_change.py`); the reviewer's probes are ported there.

**Postings and concurrency (review 1, 5, 12, 17).** Every service that posts or frees money (run approval and reversal, payment recording and approval, final settlement approval) takes the entity's payroll lock (its `PayrollSettings` row, `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`) and then the document rows, before it reads an open amount; two salary or GOSI payments, or a payment and a reversal, approved at the same moment never both pass (PostgreSQL thread tests). Loan approval locks the employee row before the 3 x BASIC check. `reverse_run` is refused (en/ar) while a later run deducted a remainder carried from one of its installments or the loan was rescheduled since; while a GOSI payment of the period covers its contributions; and once a WPS file exists for the run unless `acknowledge_wps` with a written `wps_reason` (logged in the access log). A payment's bank account is a postable asset account of the paying entity. `return_run` checks the same matrix row as approval. Employer bank settings changes (salary bank, bank account, WPS format, establishment ID, employer IBAN) are logged, masked.

**Runs net against what their period already booked (review 2, 4, 9, 10; C-A2, C-A10).** For every run (regular, off-cycle, leave advance) and the final settlement, the period's contract pay (per component), reductions and maternity top-up are netted against the lines of approved runs that belong to the period (its own runs and later lines with that period of origin): only the difference is paid; a smaller reduction than booked is released as a `retro` line (`release:<type>`); a pay decrease or a new reduction after the month was paid is a warning only (Art. 92), except in the final settlement where salary paid beyond the last working day is recovered from the final dues (Art. 88). GOSI of the period is charged only on the wage not yet covered (the period's payslips' GOSI wage plus later retro deltas).
- **Off-cycle runs** pay only what is still due for the named employees (a payslip refused in the regular run, carried maternity days now confirmed or refused) and the inputs not yet taken; no loans, no EOSB, no re-examination of earlier months; an employee with nothing due is left out (count warning); off-cycle runs of the `out_of_country` group are refused until built.
- **Day weights (review 4):** unpaid, sick and maternity reductions use the same day weights as the earnings (day 31 carries nothing on the 30-day basis; the last day of February carries the rest of a 30-day month), and are booked per component so the WPS fields can be net (C-C2). A whole month of unpaid or carried leave reduces exactly the month.
- **Inputs (review 9):** an input records the run that took it (`taken_run`, or `final_settlement`); a run takes the approved inputs of its period or earlier that no approved run or settlement took yet, so an input approved after its period's run goes to the next run; a reversal frees them; an input taken meanwhile by another run forces "recalculate".
- **Earlier months (review 10):** every month of the group with an approved regular run, from the hire month, in which the employee was employed is re-examined (also months without a payslip: a contract approved late, a refused payslip not yet paid off-cycle), with the month's reductions when nothing was booked for it.
- **Refused payslips (C-A2):** `submit_run(exclude_refused=True)` submits without them (R56 amended); a pay date more than a month after the previous run's pay date (30 days, or the same day of the next month when later) is an en/ar warning (Labour Law Art. 90).
- **Leave in advance (C-A10, Art. 109):** `prepare_leave_advance(leave)` creates, for an approved annual leave of a KSA employee, one off-cycle run (purpose `leave_advance`, pay date the day before the leave) per period of the leave whose default pay date is after the leave starts, paying the leave days' contract pay; the regular run pays the rest of the month net of it and the full GOSI. The alert job tells the payroll officers 14 days ahead while no advance exists.

**Stored rows are what is posted (review 6, 5, 15).** A run stores a hash of its stored payslips and lines right after the calculation (`stored_hash`); approval refuses a mismatch (`payslips_changed`, logged denial). Guarded payroll models use their guarded manager as `_base_manager` (`base_manager_name = "objects"`) and refuse `update`, `delete`, `bulk_create` and `bulk_update`. PostgreSQL (migrations 0005, 0010): payslips and lines locked once the run is submitted; deducted (or settled-in-final, outstanding-after-exit) installments never deleted or changed, except back to planned when their run is reversed; WPS files never rewritten (bank-processed file and burn nomination set once); final settlements locked once approved and their lines once submitted.

**Admin (review 3).** `payroll/admin.py` subclasses `core.admin.EntityScopedAdmin` (memberships, never `is_superuser`), read-only; inputs and loans need `payroll.view_payslip`, WPS files `payroll.generate_wps_file`; WPS content, bank-processed content and exceptions, employer bank details and run employee notes are never shown; `MaternityGosiCase` (health data) and payslips are not registered.

**WPS and Mudad (review 7, 8, 15; C-C1 to C-C3, C-C5).** Every CSV cell starting with `= + - @`, tab or carriage return is prefixed with an apostrophe; hr refuses such names and bank names at entry. A payslip stores a keyed hash of the IBAN and the approved termination known at calculation (both in the approval fingerprint); a WPS file lists as exceptions an employee whose IBAN changed or whose termination was approved since. `mudad_placeholder`: MOL-BAS = this period's BASIC net of its reductions, MOL-HALI = cash HOUSING net of its reductions, MOL-OEA = every other earning net of reductions (never negative; MOL-ALL = gross); a total-deductions column named by `PayrollSettings.wps_deductions_field` (default `DEDUCTIONS`, **[CC-verify: field name per bank]**); DEST-ID = the BIC of the salary bank and BANK-57 = the BIC of the employee's IBAN bank from the new parameter `wps.bank_codes` (Saudi IBAN bank identifier to BIC, `unverified`, **[CC-verify: WPS bank code per bank, P18]**; an IBAN bank not in it is an exception); the salary bank must be set. The bank-processed file is attached once, at most `PAYROLL_BANK_FILE_MAX_BYTES` (default 5,000,000), and its hash is copied on the `MudadSubmission`. `WpsFile.generated_at` comes from the service clock. Mudad alerts are sent 7, 10 and 15 days after the run's pay date (R53 amended: no longer days 7/10/15 of the following month).

**IBAN control in hr (review 8).** An active employee's IBAN or bank changes only through `hr.request_iban_change`, approved by a second holder of `hr.manage_employee` (`hr.approve_iban_change`; never the requester or the employee), or rejected; requests, approvals and draft edits are logged with the last four characters only (hr.md "As built").

**Bai' ajel (review 11).** `record_asset_acquisition(contract, purchase_bill, possession_date, possession_evidence_ref)` after the terms: the bill must be a posted invoicing bill of the entity debiting 1250 at exactly the contract cost, possession on or after the bill date with evidence; status `asset_acquired`. `approve_deferred_sale` is refused before it, if the bill is no longer posted at the cost, or with `sale_date` before possession. ~~Account 1250 stays outside the chart template~~ *Amended 2026-10-20:* 1241, 1250 and 4610 are in the chart template (ledger 0021, CFO-approved P19(g)).

**Reports (review 13, D1).** `gosi_report` gives totals per branch to `payroll.view_cost_reports`; the per-employee rows only to a holder of `payroll.view_payslip`, and that view is logged. A run's `warnings` hold counts only; per-employee details are on the payslips and in `employee_notes` (codes; not in the admin).

**Smaller fixes (review 16, 17; C-A4, C-A7, C-A13, C-B4).** `_eosb` turns only the hr refusals `no_terms` and `remuneration_no_eosb` into warnings; any other refusal stops the calculation. A local entity employee left out of a run is a count warning. Court-ordered deductions are capped at a quarter of the month's wage due and damage recovery at 5 days' wage a month for all inputs of that basis together, at entry (same work period) and at calculation (counting what the period's approved runs took) (Art. 92(6), 91). Art. 106 overtime has a case: `inventory_closing` (106(1)) is at most `overtime.art106_inventory_max_days` = 30 distinct days a year (new parameter, `unverified`). The payslip is Arabic first (`lang="ar" dir="rtl"`) with "النص العربي هو المعتمد / The Arabic text prevails", paid days, pay date and GOSI wage. The multi-employer cap note no longer shows the other employers' wage.

**GOSI booking (C-B7).** The full GOSI share is booked to 2340 every month. The part of the employee share that the net pay cannot cover (the 50% cap, a month of unpaid or carried leave) is a `gosi_uncollected` line: for a Saudi `new_m273` employee a receivable from the employee (Dr 1230 / Cr 2340), recovered by a later run as a `gosi_catch_up` deduction credited to 1230, within the caps, with one `GosiCatchUpNotice` per month of origin (R58, P19(j)); for `legacy_m33` employer cost (Dr 5110 / Cr 2340, SIL M/33 Art. 19(4)). A missed share found later is booked the same way. A GOSI payment may go up to the GOSI invoice recorded in `GosiReconciliation` for the period; the part above the ERP contributions is an en/ar warning on the payment.

**Final settlement (C-A11, review 14; 3.8, R28, R34, R49, E5).** Built as `FinalSettlement` / `FinalSettlementLine` **[Reg]** with permissions `payroll.prepare_final_settlement` (payroll_officer) and `payroll.approve_final_settlement` (A, P; approver, MP fallback; access seed **V14**, migration `core.0023`; matrix activity `payroll.final_settlement`).
- hr's termination approval drafts it through `payroll.hr_port.PayrollHrPort.create_draft` (KSA employees only: remuneration staff are paid by the run for their last month, H17); a recalculated EOSB points the open draft at the new input; a withdrawn termination discards it; `is_approved` tells hr when it is approved (EOSB recalculation and withdrawal then refused, hr R40, R41). The draft's creator is the termination's drafter.
- `prepare_final_settlement` (lines replaced): salary to the last working day (`_calc_ksa` final mode) net of what the runs paid for the leaving month (salary paid beyond the last day is an `other_deduction` recovered from the dues, up to them); earlier months re-examined (retro); every approved input not yet taken; GOSI of the leaving month on days of service (Regs Art. 20(2), R66), with a warning when the run booked more; leave encashment from hr (Art. 111); notice pay when the employer gave short notice (Art. 76; a worker's short notice is a warning, never deducted, Art. 92); Art. 77 compensation from hr; the EOSB award of the hr snapshot (fraction per hr R37; mutual agreement at the full statutory award) and a `separate_settlement` above it only for a mutual agreement with `written_consent_ref` (P19(i)); then, without the monthly 10% / 50% caps (P17), loan recovery from all final dues (from the non-EOSB dues only if `PAYROLL_EOSB_DEDUCTION_ALLOWED` is False) and bai' ajel recovery (EOSB only with the final-dues undertaking), the rest an en/ar warning (loan write-off refused until OQ-7; bai' ajel stays on 1240 as `outstanding_after_exit`).
- `submit_final_settlement`, `return_final_settlement`, `approve_final_settlement` (not the preparer, submitter or the employee; recomputed and fingerprint-checked; stored-lines hash checked; number `{entity}-FS-{year}-{000001}`; entry dated the last working day through the registry): E5 — earnings Dr their accounts, EOSB Dr 2500 for the employee's provision balance with the shortfall Dr 5120 or the excess released Cr 5120 (sub-ledger `utilisation` and `release` rows bring the balance to zero), employer GOSI Dr 5110 / Cr 2340, employee GOSI Cr 2340, recoveries Cr 1230 / 1240, net Cr 2330; installments `settled_in_final` (a partly recovered bai' ajel installment is split, the rest `outstanding_after_exit`), loans settled when nothing is left, inputs taken. `pending_documents` keeps `close_period` from closing a period with a submitted settlement.
- Payment: `record_payment(kind="final_settlement", final_settlement=...)`, at most the open net, approved by the approver: Dr 2330 / Cr bank; the settlement is `paid` when fully paid.
- Deadline (Art. 88): `due_date` = last working day + 7 days, + 14 when the worker ended the contract (from hr); the alert job warns the payroll officers and approvers 3 days before, on the due day and the day after while unpaid.

### 10.6 Re-review fixes of the final settlement (2026-10-20, as built)
Built on the same branch from the re-review of §10.5, on the owner's instruction "proceed on recommendations, no questions". Regression tests: `payroll/test_rereview_settlement.py` (the reviewer's probes P1 to P11 ported) and `payroll/test_pg_concurrency.py` (PostgreSQL races). Migrations `payroll.0011`, `hr.0020`.

1. **Settlement lines are booked (R14, R58; review 2, 10).** The lines of an approved (or paid) final settlement count in what a period already booked, by their period of origin or, without one, the leaving month: contract pay and reductions in the netting, GOSI wage covered, shares advanced and recovered. Each line stores the payslip line type it was computed as (`FinalSettlementLine.slip_line_type`); salary paid beyond the last working day and recovered from the final dues (`overpaid:`) counts as less pay for the month. A run (any kind) whose period is on or before the leaving month of an employee with an approved settlement leaves that employee out (amended by §10.7 item 1; was: the whole run refused): corrections after the settlement are a later document, not a run (the leaving month's regular run already leaves the leaver out, R10).
2. **Run reversal (R13).** `reverse_run` is refused (`settlement_netted`) while a submitted, approved or paid settlement of one of its payslip employees has its leaving month on or after the run's period, or has a line with the run's period as origin. A calculated or returned settlement of such an employee goes back to `draft` (lines and hashes cleared, warning "recalculate"), so it cannot be submitted before it is recalculated.
3. **Header in the hash (review 6).** The stored hash covers every line and the header fields the entry and the effects are built from (`SETTLEMENT_HEADER`: totals, EOSB award and provision balance, separate settlement, settlement input, EOSB calculation, inputs taken, period, last working day), read from the database; approval also compares the stored totals with the recomputed ones. A mismatch refuses (`payslips_changed`, denial logged). PostgreSQL: the header is locked once submitted; the only changes allowed are submitted → returned (with its reason), → discarded, → approved (number, approver, time), then the journal entry once and approved → paid.
4. **Settlement GOSI is payable (R29, R23).** The settlement's 2340 credits (both shares, a share borne by the employer, an advanced employee share) count under the **month of the settlement's journal entry** (the leaving month, or the next open month when it was closed: §10.7 item 3) in `_gosi_booked`, `_gosi_open` (GOSI payments and the reconciliation), `gosi_report` (branches and, for `payroll.view_payslip`, the employee row) and the GOSI guard of `reverse_run`.
5. **Notices (R58, P19(j)).** A GOSI share recovered by the final settlement gets its `GosiCatchUpNotice` (now `final_settlement` FK, `run` optional, one of them required).
6. **Payroll closed before the settlement.** Approval is refused (`settlement_runs_open`) while a month from the hire month to the month before leaving has no approved regular in-country run, or a run up to the leaving month is submitted (earlier months, or the leaving month with the employee in it). *Default:* the months start at the later of the hire month and the entity's first approved regular run (payroll went live then; earlier months were paid outside the ERP and come in as opening balances); an entity with no approved run yet is refused until its first regular run is approved (§10.7 item 1; was: requires none). Any run up to the leaving month that is not approved yet and has a payslip for the employee also refuses (§10.7 item 1). A run never accrues EOSB for an employee with an approved settlement (warning).
7. **Bai' ajel (R47).** One purchase bill backs one contract: `record_asset_acquisition` refuses a bill already used by another contract, and the database has a unique constraint on the bill (`payroll_sale_one_purchase_bill`).
8. **Partial loan recovery (R28, OQ-7).** Only installments with a recovered part are `settled_in_final`; a partly recovered installment is split (the unrecovered part a new installment carried from it); the rest is `outstanding_after_exit` (new `LoanInstallment` status, final like the bai' ajel one), on 1230 until the write-off decision (OQ-7). The loan stays active while anything is outstanding.
9. **Notice pay (Art. 76)** = actual wage × shortfall days / the day divisor as one fraction, rounded once in hr (`FinalSettlementInput.notice_shortfall_wage`, hr "As built" 2026-10-20).
10. **Separate settlement** amounts are SAR with at most 2 decimals (`fx.has_places`), else refused (`separate_settlement`).
11. **Closed month.** The settlement entry is dated the last working day when its fiscal period is open, else the first day of the next open period (as a run reversal); the EOSB sub-ledger rows carry the entry date (§10.7 item 3; was: the last working day). A submitted settlement still keeps `close_period` from closing its month.
12. **Draft gate.** The draft is created for `ksa_employee` staff only; a KSA employee's termination approval is refused (en/ar, `payroll_not_set_up`) while the entity has no `PayrollSettings`.
13. **Admin.** `PayrollPaymentAdmin` lists and shows final settlement payments only to holders of `payroll.view_payslip` in the payment's entity.
14. **CSV.** A cell is also prefixed with an apostrophe when a formula character follows leading whitespace.
15. **PostgreSQL races.** Two payments of one settlement approved at once (one passes, the other `overpayment`) and two approvals of one settlement at once (one posts, the other `settlement_status`) are thread tests.

### 10.7 Verification fixes of the final settlement (2026-10-20, as built)
Built on the same branch from the verification review of §10.6, on the owner's instruction "proceed on recommendations, no questions". Regression tests: `payroll/test_verification_settlement.py` (the reviewer's probes B1, B2 and B3 ported; B3 runs on SQLite and PostgreSQL); `payroll/test_rereview_settlement.py` and `payroll/test_pg_concurrency.py` adjusted. No migration.

1. **Runs and the settlement (B1, B2).** *Skip, not refuse:* a run (any kind) whose period is on or before the leaving month of an employee with an approved (or paid) settlement leaves that employee out; the run is calculated for the others with a count warning on the run ("N employee(s) left out: an approved final settlement closed their payroll", en/ar) and an employee note with the code and the settlement number (code `employee_settled` is no longer raised). The skip is part of the run fingerprint, so a run calculated before a settlement was approved is refused at its approval (`recalculate`). *Runs not approved yet:* approval of the settlement is refused (`settlement_runs_open`, en/ar) while any run up to the leaving month in draft, calculated, returned or submitted status has a payslip (not refused) for the employee; a recalculation of the leaving month leaves the leaver out (R10), an earlier month is approved first. Submitted runs of earlier months still refuse as in §10.6 item 6. *First run:* while the entity has no approved regular in-country run, approval is refused (`settlement_runs_open`, en/ar "approve its first regular run (the months before leaving, or the go-live month)"); once any regular run is approved, §10.6 item 6 applies from it (a go-live in the leaving month is enough: that run leaves the leaver out).
2. **Stored lines against the recalculation (B3).** Approval also compares the stored settlement lines, read from the database in creation order, with the recomputed lines, both in the `_settlement_fingerprint` line format (line type, amount, account code, component, GOSI branch, quantity, rate, source, period of origin, computed-as type; amounts at their stored precision) and hashed (`_settlement_lines_hash`). A line edited in the database with `stored_hash` recomputed is refused (`payslips_changed`, denial logged), on SQLite and PostgreSQL.
3. **Closed leaving month: tie-outs by the entry date.** When the leaving month is closed and the entry posts on the first day of the next open month (§10.6 item 11): the settlement's 2340 credits count under the month of the entry date in `_gosi_booked`, `_gosi_open`, GOSI payments, `record_gosi_reconciliation`, `gosi_report` and the reversal guard (the GOSI invoice of the leaving month may then exceed the ERP figure of that month by the leaver's contributions; the payment allowance up to the recorded GOSI invoice and its warning cover it); the EOSB sub-ledger `utilisation` and `release` rows are dated the entry date, and the R25 tie-out compares account 2500 with the sub-ledger rows dated on or before the same date. With the leaving month open the entry date is the last working day and nothing changes.

### 10.8 As built: slice 2 (2026-10-20)
Built on the same branch on the owner's instruction "start slice 2 now, proceed on the team's recommendations, no questions". Regression tests: `payroll/test_slice2_settlement.py`, `test_slice2_costs.py`, `test_slice2_item4.py`, `test_slice2_egypt.py`, `test_slice2_bai_ajel.py`, `test_slice2_bai_ajel_real.py` (the real invoicing port), `payroll/test_payments.py` (WHT return), `invoicing/test_wht_return_sources.py`, `hr/test_jurisdiction_activation.py`. Migrations `payroll.0012` (5930), `0013` (documents), `0014` (PostgreSQL guards), `0015` (Egypt rows), `0016` (bai' ajel status), `hr.0021`; permissions in access seed **V16** (`core.0025`).

1. **Final settlement correction (R34; CLAUDE.md rule 1).** An approved (or paid) settlement is never edited or reversed: `create_settlement_correction(settlement, lines=[...], reason_ar=...)` (`payroll.prepare_settlement_correction`, payroll officer) records **difference lines only** (`increase` / `decrease` of `salary_to_last_day`, `retro`, `leave_encashment`, `eosb`, `notice_pay`, `separate_settlement` (mutual agreement with written consent only), `other_earning`, `other_deduction`, `employee_gosi` and `employer_gosi` with their branch); loan, bai' ajel and GOSI-share recoveries are not corrected here (their sub-ledgers moved at approval). A decrease never takes a line type below what the settlement and earlier corrections booked; one open correction per settlement; nothing that cancels out. `approve_settlement_correction` (`payroll.approve_settlement_correction`, A, P; approver, MP fallback; never the preparer, submitter or the employee; stored lines hash-checked, limits checked again) posts its own entry dated the approval day: earnings Dr their account (5100 or the component's), EOSB on 5120 (the provision was used up), deductions the other way, employer GOSI Dr 5110 / Cr 2340, the change of the net on 2330; number `{entity}-FSC-{year}-{000001}`. The open net of the settlement (payments) includes approved corrections; a decrease below what was paid is an en/ar warning (recovered only with written consent or a judgment, Art. 92, outside payroll). Correction GOSI counts in the month of its entry for GOSI payments, the reconciliation and the GOSI report. **Statement:** `render_settlement_statement` (Arabic first, "the Arabic text prevails", a DRAFT mark before approval) shows the stored lines, employer contributions, every approved correction, payments and the balance; `payroll.view_payslip`, each view logged.
2. **Loan write-off on exit (OQ-7 default, R28).** After the leaver's settlement is approved, the loan balance it did not cover (installments `outstanding_after_exit`) is written off: `prepare_loan_write_off` (payroll officer, Arabic reason) → `approve_loan_write_off` (`payroll.approve_loan_write_off`, A, P) by the entity's top authority from the matrix seam (`payroll.approvals.TOP_AUTHORITY_ROWS`: `managing_partner` in the group parent, `ceo` elsewhere; the `ceo` role comes with the access build, so a subsidiary's write-off is refused until then) → Dr expense / Cr 1230, dated the approval day, number `LWO`; installments `written_off`, loan `written_off`, an access-log row. The expense account is the setting `PayrollSettings.loan_write_off_account` (an expense of the entity, never Sharia-prohibited; empty = 5100) **[CFO to confirm the account]**. The amount is checked again at approval; one open write-off per loan.
3. **Employee cost items and the fully loaded cost report (R35, R63; billing R71).** `create_cost_item` (`payroll.manage_cost_items`) per employee: kind (`medical_insurance`, `iqama_renewal`, `work_permit`, `visa`, `other_government_fee`, `other_benefit`), amount, Arabic description and **one posted source document of the entity** (vendor bill or journal entry); its month is the source's accounting month (billing OQ-37 default); the items on one source never exceed the cost it booked (its debits other than input VAT 1310). Approved by the approver (A); posts nothing. `prepare_cost_report(entity, period)` after the month's regular run is approved: per secondment, days on secondment, and prorated gross, employer GOSI, EOSB accrual of the month's approved payslips and the approved cost items by kind; submitted and approved (A, not the preparer; refused "recalculate" if a source changed); a later version supersedes the approved one. **Billing seam:** `payroll.billing_port.PayrollCostPort.approved_cost(assignment, period)` returns the approved total (or None: billing keeps the period `held`). Billing's `recharge_basis` is not built, so billing does not call it yet (no billing code changed). OQ-48 warning on KSA-to-KSA secondments.
4. **Item 4.** (a) *Carried maternity days:* when the hr officer records GOSI's decision, the payroll officers get an alert naming the months with carried pay; `prepare_maternity_settlement(case)` creates and calculates one off-cycle run per such month (refused case: the carried pay released; confirmed: the top-up); not twice while one waits. (b) *Remuneration retro and GOSI catch-up:* earlier `out_of_country` months are re-examined like KSA ones: retro pay per component in the contract currency at this run's rate; for a subordinate Saudi, annuities on the retro wage at that month's rate (employee share deducted now), a missed share caught up now with a `GosiCatchUpNotice` (`new_m273`) or borne by the employer (`legacy_m33`, Art. 19(4)); no Saudi caps (R41). (c) *GOSI fine (E14):* `record_gosi_fine` (payroll officer; GOSI invoice reference; the ERP estimate 2% per month or part from the 15th of the following month is stored beside GOSI's figure, which governs) → `approve_gosi_fine` (approver, A, P): Dr 5930 / Cr 2340 dated the fine date (or the next open period); the fine counts in the period's GOSI payable and report; 5930 added to the chart (CFO-approved, P19(g)).
