### 11.10 As built: slice 3 items 1–3 (2026-10-20)

Built by the module-builder on the owner's instruction "start payroll slice 3", with every recommended default of 11.9 (OQ-49 to OQ-56) adopted. **Item 4 (R82 to R84) is not built:** the shared authority matrix / approval engine is built in parallel; `payroll/approvals.py` and the core/access approval code are unchanged, and payroll's seam (§10.1) still decides every approval. Tests: `payroll/test_slice3_off_cycle.py`, `test_slice3_reports.py`, `test_slice3_mudad.py`, `test_slice3_pg.py` (PostgreSQL only). Migrations `payroll.0021` (models and fields) and `payroll.0022` (parameter rows, copy of recorded justifications, PostgreSQL guards); permissions in access seed **V17** (`core.0027`, chained after `core.0026`; the parallel engine build may add its own seed version, to be renumbered at merge).

1. **Off-cycle runs, out-of-country and Egypt (R69 to R74; E16, E17).** `create_run(kind="off_cycle")` accepts the `out_of_country` group and an EG entity's in-country group, with `reason_ar` and named employees of that group only (`off_cycle_group`); the KSA purposes (leave advance, maternity settlement) are refused there (`run_kind`); an EG out-of-country run is refused (`run_kind`). Refused (`regular_run_open`, en/ar) until the period's regular run of the same entity and group is approved, checked at preparation, calculation and approval. The run pays only what is still due: remuneration per component in the contract currency net of the period's approved payslips (a contract currency other than an approved payslip's is refused naming both); Egypt through the §10.5 netting; inputs approved and not yet taken whose work period (or paid-in period) is the run's period or earlier, so not bound by the cut-off; no loans, bai' ajel, EOSB or re-examination of earlier months; nothing due = left out with the count warning. **Entry date (R70, OQ-49):** set at calculation: the last day of the period when its fiscal period is open, else the first day of the next open period (`entry_date_reason=period_closed`, en/ar warning, rate of that date); approval refuses (`recalculate`) when the entry date would differ. **GOSI (R71):** a subordinate Saudi's annuities on the month's `REMUNERATION` in SAR not yet covered by an approved payslip of the period (floor and cap on the month); one-off items carry none (OQ-50). **WPS (R72, R74):** `generate_wps_file` is refused (`wps_not_applicable`, en/ar) for every out-of-country run and every Egypt run (a regular out-of-country run produced an empty file before). WHT stays at payment (R43; the payment names the payslips; base = gross in the share of the net paid). **Egypt (R73, R74):** EG rows only, on the month combined: social insurance and UHI shares on the month's insurable wage less what the period booked (a month at the maximum gets none; the UHI employer minimum never twice); Martyrs' Fund on this run's gross; salary tax cumulative over the year including the period's other approved payslips (a negative result is not refunded, en/ar warning: the next regular run trues it up, OQ-51); the Egypt caps on the combined month; activation as the regular Egypt run (calculated with a warning, refused at submission and approval while a row read is unverified). An employee with an approved termination or a contract ending in or before the period is refused (`eg_final_settlement_not_built`) at preparation and, if found later, at calculation. Overtime inputs stay refused at entry.
2. **Liability reports (R75 to R78).** `leave_liability_report(entity, as_of, *, user)` and `eosb_provision_report(...)` (`payroll.prepare_liability_report`; payroll officer, accountant) store a `PayrollLiabilityReport` with its lines and post nothing (no entry, no `EosbProvisionEntry`, no `LeaveLedger` row; 2350 / 5130 unused). `as_of` after today (Riyadh) is refused (`as_of_future`). The header carries the en/ar basis (date, Art. 2, 84, 111, P19(h), "report only: nothing is posted", hr OQ-6, the excluded groups with counts, the parameter ids read, "the Arabic text prevails"), the parameter and contract-version ids and a SHA-256 of header and lines; a later report of the same entity, kind and date supersedes (`superseded`, `superseded_by`), the only change a sealed report takes (model guard; PostgreSQL trigger; lines append-only). Reading: `get_liability_report` (entity scope, another entity's report 404) and `liability_report_data` (totals with `payroll.view_cost_reports` or `payroll.view_payslip`; lines only with `payroll.view_payslip`, each such view logged; a stored snapshot whose hash no longer matches is refused, `report_changed`); `render_liability_report` gives HTML (Arabic first) or CSV (codes only). **Leave (R76, OQ-52):** KSA employees: ledger rows dated on or before `as_of` + the open month's pro rata (hr's accrual fraction to `as_of`, in memory, only while that month has no accrual row dated on or before `as_of`) + approved annual leave after `as_of` whose taken rows are dated on or before it; value = untaken days (2 dp) x daily wage (Σ `counts_for_eosb` / `leave.encashment` day divisor 30); remuneration staff and settled employees excluded with reasons; Egypt: days shown, `not_valued` until `leave.encashment_basis` (EG) is verified and states `wage_flag` and `day_divisor`; other jurisdictions excluded. **EOSB (R77, OQ-53):** KSA employees employed on `as_of`: `hr._compute_eosb(..., resignation)` gives the Art. 84 full award (the figure) and the Art. 85 resignation award (memo); the employee's sub-ledger balance (memo) and account 2500 on the date (header memo); `no_terms` / `remuneration_no_eosb` give `not_valued`, any other hr refusal stops the report; remuneration, settled, left-not-yet-settled and non-SA staff excluded; an Egypt report states "no statutory end-of-service award configured" (en/ar) with no amounts.
3. **Mudad justifications (R79 to R81).** `MudadJustification` rows on payslips of approved in-country runs of SA entities, KSA employees only (`mudad_not_applicable` for remuneration and Egypt). Suggestions (`source=system`, status `suggested`, created by the person whose action triggered them) are written at WPS generation and when the bank-processed file is attached, and on demand (`suggest_mudad_justifications`): WPS exceptions (IBAN: `iban_exception`; termination approved since: `final_settlement`), bank rejections (`bank_rejected`, evidence `RET-CDE <code>`), refused payslips (`paid_off_cycle` if an approved off-cycle payslip of the period exists, else `payroll_correction`, non-payment), gross below the contract monthly wage (reason from the lines: maternity, unpaid leave, sick tier, joiner / leaver as `partial_payment`, else `payroll_correction`), and a late pay date (Art. 90, `late_payment` with reason `other` and a system text); one live row per payslip and reason. `record_mudad_justification` (`payroll.record_mudad_justification`, payroll officer) records a `draft` by hand; `confirm_mudad_justification` turns a suggestion into a `draft` (edits allowed); `approve_mudad_justification` (`payroll.approve_mudad_justification`, A; seam roles `hr_officer` for activity `payroll.mudad_justification`, so the MP's fallback permission is refused) never by its creator, last modifier or the employee, and fills the portal option from `wps.mudad.justification_options` (11.11, C3); `record_mudad_notice` sets `due_date` = notice + 10 days; `record_justification_submission` (`payroll.record_mudad_submission`) sets `submitted_on`, `mudad_reference` and `worker_response_due` = + 3 days; `record_justification_outcome` records accepted / rejected and the worker's response; `withdraw_mudad_justification` with an Arabic reason (rows are never deleted; final statuses never change; PostgreSQL trigger). Texts with an obvious diagnosis or health word (en/ar list) are refused (`justification_invalid`, the instruction en/ar). Alerts (`PayrollAlert` kind `mudad_justification`, en/ar) to payroll officers and hr_officers 3 days before the due date, on it and the day after while not submitted (daily `payroll_alerts` job). `export_mudad_justifications(run, *, user, format_code="csv"|"html")` (`payroll.generate_wps_file`) lists the approved (and submitted, accepted) rows with MOL-ID, refreshes their portal options from the parameter while approved, warns en/ar per row without a code, is stored encrypted as `MudadJustificationExport` (kept like WPS files; nominated for a burn decision after 10 years) and logged. ~~WPS columns (R81)~~ *Removed 2026-10-21 (11.11, C3): no salary file carries justifications.* `MudadSubmission.justifications` is read-only: `payroll.0022` copies its entries into rows (`source=import`, `submitted`). Every Mudad code stays **[verify]**; the parameter is seeded empty and unverified (OQ-56).

**Fixes found on the way (with tests):** a regular remuneration payslip now records the inputs it pays (`input_ids` was empty, so a later run could pay them again); `approve_run` repeats the calculation's Art. 90 pay-date warning (without it a run with a late pay date never matched its fingerprint and could not be approved); the AT-26 access scan now filters dot directories below the repository only (in a `.claude/worktrees` checkout it scanned nothing).

**Deviations and builder defaults (to review):**
- AT-79: EH (hr_officer) cannot generate the EGY report: 11.7 gives `payroll.prepare_liability_report` to the payroll officer and the accountant only; the test uses the EGY payroll officer and asserts EH is refused.
- AT-80: hr dates an annual leave's `taken` row on the leave's first day, so "a taken row dated 2027-05-02" for a leave from 2027-05-20 does not arise from hr; the test reproduces it by patching the date, and a second test shows hr's own dating gives the same 15.85 days (the days stay in the balance).
- AT-83 / 11.3.2: `paid_amount` is the payslip **gross** for amount differences and partial payments (R79(d), AT-83), 0 for non-payment; 11.3.2 said "net".
- R79(d): only a gross **below** the contract wage is suggested (overtime and bonuses above it are not justified); R79(e) uses reason `other` with a system text; `payroll_correction` also takes `non_payment` (R79(c)).
- R81 "S!": the export uses `payroll.generate_wps_file`, which is not S! (§10.2 default); no extra segregation is enforced.
- Bank-processed file (R79(b)): read as CSV, header row naming `RET-CDE`, employees matched by `MOL-ID` or `employee_code`; an empty or all-zero code is a success **[verify: each bank's return codes, P18]**.
- R70 "each payslip has its own fingerprint": the run fingerprint and stored hash cover every payslip and line (§10.5); there is no per-payslip hash field.
- R73(a): `si.insurable_components` has `one_off_inputs: false`, so inputs are never insurable; the combined month is the contract's insurable wage.
- E17 / AT-76, AT-77 run in April 2027 with a 2027 `si.wage_limits` row entered in the test (NOSI publishes the year's limits each November; none is seeded for 2027). AT-76's refused regular payslip and R74's late exit are produced by patching the calculation in the test.
- AT-78: the leaver case is tested with a fixed-term contract ending in the period; an approved Egypt termination is not exercised (hr's Egypt termination path is not built).
- Egypt leave pro rata is not computed (hr has no Egypt accrual rows, OQ-26); a leave type `ANNUAL` must exist in the Egypt entity (hr seeds none for EG yet).
- The EOSB report's `total_days` is 0 (days belong to the leave report); an employee whose last working day is before `as_of` without an approved settlement is excluded ("left").
- No cross-entity total is built (R75: one entity per report).
- Mudad approval goes through payroll's seam with the role list in `payroll.services` (`MUDAD_ROLES`), because `payroll/approvals.py` is not changed in this slice; item 4 registers the activity with the engine.
- Admin: read-only, scoped entries for justifications (`payroll.view_payslip`), exports (`payroll.generate_wps_file`, content never shown) and liability reports (`payroll.view_cost_reports`).

**Test runs (2026-10-20/21):** full suite on SQLite 2160 passed, 107 skipped; slice 3 and payroll PostgreSQL tests on a throwaway PostgreSQL 16 cluster 30 passed; `core`, `banking` and `billing` on PostgreSQL 477 passed. Two full PostgreSQL runs on a heavily loaded shared machine hit the 2-hour limit at about 99 % with one unidentified failure each in the first 15 % (banking, billing, core), which all pass when run on their own on PostgreSQL; to recheck on an unloaded machine.

Still open: item 4 (R82 to R84) after the engine merges; OQ-10 / P18 bank formats; OQ-56 Mudad code list and whether justifications travel in the upload file; OQ-55 Egypt leave basis; OQ-51 Egypt bonus tax and SI; hr OQ-6 leave accrual.

### 11.11 Review and compliance fixes of slice 3 (2026-10-21, as built)

From the independent code review and the compliance check of slice 3 (official HRSD WPS guide: justifications are chosen from a drop-down on the Mudad portal, due 10 days after the upload, the worker has 3 days), on the owner's instruction to adopt the recommendations. Where this section differs from 11.10, this section governs. Migration `payroll.0023` (fields, backfill, PostgreSQL guard); tests in the four `test_slice3_*.py` files.

Code review:
1. **No month paid twice.** `reverse_run` refuses a regular out-of-country or Egypt run while an approved off-cycle run of the same entity, period and group exists (`off_cycle_approved`, en/ar: reverse the off-cycle run first). Every remuneration and Egypt run (regular too) now nets the period against its approved payslips: remuneration per component in the contract currency; Egypt salary through the §10.5 netting, social insurance and UHI shares less what the period booked (the UHI employer minimum never twice).
2. **No GOSI on an FX difference (R71; compliance C5).** A remuneration payslip charges GOSI only while the month's `REMUNERATION` is still due in the contract currency, and only on branches no approved payslip of the period booked.
3. **Backfill.** `payroll.0023` sets `taken_run` on inputs named by lines (`input:<pk>`) of approved, non-reversed remuneration payslips from before slice 3.
4. **Justification content frozen** from approval (model guard and PostgreSQL trigger): while approved only the portal option, the export link, the submission fields, the withdrawal and the notice / due dates change; after submission only Mudad's answer and the worker's response.
5. **Liability report totals need `payroll.view_cost_reports`** (R75); lines also `payroll.view_payslip`.
6. **EOSB report leavers:** a leaver without an approved settlement is valued at the last working day with the actual termination kind's fraction ("due, settlement pending", en/ar); a settlement counts only when its last working day is on or before the as-at date (both reports).
7. **Bank returns:** an empty ID never matches an empty `MOL-ID`; WPS exceptions carry a reason code (`no_iban`, `iban_changed`, `termination`, `bank_code`) and the termination case is read from it.
8. **Health words:** parameter `mudad.health_words` (SA, team list, **[verify: DPO]**), the evidence reference is screened too; "عملية" alone and "حمل" are no longer flagged (only "عملية جراحية"); "طبي", "medical", "injury", "إصابة", "نفسي" added; imported rows are flagged (`health_flag`), never refused.
9. **Tests added:** UHI employer minimum once per month in a UHI-live governorate (with a test minimum that binds), partial SI netting on a back-dated raise, AT-81 exact figures (37,078.77 and 24,719.18), AT-78 with an approved Egypt termination (stood in for at payroll's lookup: hr cannot approve an Egypt termination yet, its Egypt rows such as `leave.sick` are not configured, OQ-26), the last modifier of a justification never approves it.

Compliance:
- **C1 Egypt salary tax (OQ-57, Egypt tax adviser):** regular pay is annualised (year to date x 12 / months); bonuses and one-off earnings (`Payslip.taxable_irregular`) are added once and their tax is due in full in the month paid. Contract pay paid by an off-cycle run stays regular (otherwise E17's refused regular month would bear no tax); only its bonus and one-off lines are irregular.
- **C2:** a justification's due date defaults to the run's recorded Mudad upload + 10 days (Mudad's own notice overrides); an approved run with open justifications and no recorded upload gets one alert.
- **C3:** no justification columns in any salary file; the portal option (`mudad_option_ar` / `_en`, parameter `wps.mudad.justification_options`, [verify]) replaces the file code; the export is the officer's worksheet; not segregated (decided).
- **C4:** `payroll.hr_port.PayrollHrPort.eosb_additional_wage` gives the Art. 2 regular additions (12-month average of bonus lines and commission / profit-share inputs; in-kind housing at `eosb.in_kind_housing`, 3 months of BASIC a year, **[verify: legal]**); used by hr's termination snapshot, the monthly provision and both reports.
- **C6:** approving a `joiner_leaver_proration` or `final_settlement` justification records an en/ar warning while the GOSI joining registration or leaving exclusion is not recorded.
- **C7:** a late payment is a pay date more than 30 days after the previous run's.
- **C8:** the Art. 85 wording: 1/3 for 2 to 5 years inclusive, 2/3 for more than 5 (code unchanged: hr's bands were right).
- **C9:** remuneration contracts with COMMISSION or PROFIT_SHARE: refused (hr already allows only `REMUNERATION` on such a contract, R52; a test pins it).
- **C10 (OQ-58, Egypt adviser):** `si.wage_reference` (EG): `monthly` (default) or `january` (the version in force on 1 January, or a later joiner's first day).
- **C11:** proactive justifications: `record_proactive_justification(employee, ...)`, from next month or later, covering 2 to 12 months and ending at most 12 months ahead, one live per worker (database constraint); listed in the worksheet of runs in the covered months.

Not changed: `payroll/approvals.py`; KSA in-country off-cycle runs (their netting was already in place; the new reversal guard is limited to out-of-country and Egypt runs, so a KSA leave-advance run does not block reversing its regular run).

New open questions (defaults applied):

| # | Question | Owner | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-57 | Egypt salary tax on bonuses: added once in the month, not annualised; contract pay of an off-cycle month regular (C1). Also: a mid-year joiner, or months without pay, are annualised over the months paid (x 12 / months), which can over-withhold against the actual year (the annual employer settlement corrects it); is a months-worked basis required? (verification 8, no code change) | Egypt tax adviser | As built |
| OQ-58 | Egypt insurable wage: of the month or of January (C10) | Egypt adviser | `monthly` |
| OQ-59 | KSA WHT on remuneration paid to people working abroad: category and rate (OQ-29 restated) | Tax adviser | A category required on every remuneration payment; no rate assumed |
| OQ-60 | PDPL retention of justification worksheets and liability report lines (kept indefinitely like WPS files) | DPO | Kept; burn only by a human decision |
| OQ-61 | Sharia: Egypt late-payment surcharges (NOSI, ETA) when they arise, booked as a penalty expense, never interest | Sharia board | Penalty expense (no posting built yet) |
| OQ-62 | In-kind housing value in the EOSB wage (C4). The seed says 3 months of BASIC a year while GOSI uses 2 (`gosi.housing_in_kind_basic_months`): recommended to align with GOSI's 2 unless legal says otherwise. Until a person verifies `eosb.in_kind_housing`, in-kind housing counts 0 with an en/ar warning (verification 1) | Legal | 0 until verified; seed 3 [verify] |

### 11.12 Verification fixes of the slice 3 fix round (2026-10-21, as built)

From the verification review of 11.11, on the owner's instruction to adopt the recommendations. Tests: `payroll/test_slice3_verification.py` and the amended slice 3 tests. No new migration: `payroll.0023` (not yet pushed) is amended in place.

1. **R7 for the Art. 2 additions:** `PayrollHrPort.eosb_additional_wage` uses `eosb.in_kind_housing` only once a person has verified it; the row is then taken into the caller's ParamUse (`AdditionalWage.rows`, read by hr's `_compute_eosb`, recorded in the snapshot's, run's and report's parameter ids, checked under `HR_REQUIRE_VERIFIED_PARAMETERS`). Until then in-kind housing counts 0 with an en/ar warning (`AdditionalWage.warnings`: on the hr termination, the payslip of the provision, the report line). OQ-62 notes the 3 vs 2 months inconsistency.
2. **One wage:** hr's final settlement input (leave encashment, notice pay) uses the contract components counting for EOSB plus payroll's Art. 2 additions, the same wage as the award and the leave liability report (R34, R76); a test ties the settlement's daily wage to the report's.
3. **Art. 2 average:** only inputs marked `eosb_regular` (regular, contractual or customary; default not) count, and their total is divided by the months of the 12-month window the employee was employed.
4. **Back-dated raise (R71; amends 11.11 item 2):** when an approved payslip of the month exists, the month's GOSI wage is re-measured at that first payslip's rate (contract currency to SAR as booked) and the uncovered part is charged now (USD 1,000.00 raise at 3.75: annuities on 3,750.00); an FX difference alone still creates no GOSI wage because the REMUNERATION due is measured in the contract currency.
5. **`payroll.0023` data fill:** the migration installs its guard first; the guard lets exactly the one-time fill through (a row without an employee gets one, and its health flag; nothing else, final rows included), so no trigger is ever disabled; a test runs both data steps on the historical models (and, on PostgreSQL, checks that a final row stays final).
6. **Health words:** whole words only (English entries as word starts, Arabic entries as whole words after an optional clitic prefix): "تطبيق" and "طبيعي" pass, "الطبي" is refused.
7. **Settled as at:** a settlement counts only when its last working day and its approval are on or before the as-at date; a leaver whose last working day is the as-at date is valued with the termination kind's fraction.
8. OQ-57 extended (mid-year joiners, months without pay); no code change.
9. Stale text on file codes and WPS justification columns struck through (11.3.2, R81, 11.6, AT-83, 11.10).
